## lice 20 Certified Evidencelice 20 Certified Evidence

- Server route derives `canSign` only from the guarded RBAC context with `branch.daily-close.sign`.
- Workspace renders the command only for `AWAITING_SIGN_OFF` plus trusted capability.
- Password is sent only to password step-up and is excluded from sign input and saved evidence.
- Actor, tenant, reviewer identity, assurance time, drift evidence, status, audit, and event remain server-owned.
- Mobile title/close overlap was fixed and regression-tested.
- Invalid business-event source provenance was corrected to enum-valid `MANUAL` with domain metadata.
- Expanded focused verification passes 6 suites/100 tests; close regression passes 12 suites/237 tests.
- TypeScript and focused ESLint pass; service boundary has 0 active violations; role cockpit is 9/9 ready.
- Module inventory maps the page and sign action with no relevant gap; 10 unrelated warn-mode gaps remain.
- Browser certification passes 5/5 stages with zero serious/critical accessibility, overflow, clipping, overlap, page-error, or failed-request findings.
- Durable evidence proves distinct maker/checker IDs, `L1` assurance, signed audit and business event, and server-derived `SIGNED`.
- Role permissions, role codes/timestamps, and signer assurance exactly match preflight after cleanup; disposable operational counts are zero.
- Preflight: `what-next/referrals/DAILY_TRUTH_SIGN_OFF_COMMAND_UI_BROWSER_PREFLIGHT_2026-07-19.json`.
- Final

Evidence: `what-next/referrals/DAILY_TRUTH_SIGN_OFF_COMMAND_UI_BROWSER_EVIDENCE_2026-07-19.json`.

## hase 2 Promotion Decisionhase 2 Promotion Decision

The original roadmap requires at least three daily actions to be resolvable from the operating surface. Phase 2 now exposes three durable source commands: start branch daily-close review, independently sign branch daily close, and independently sign payment reconciliation.Slice 24 closed the previous promotion hold with a narrow command-state adapter, module-enforced protected action, canonical source-version check, idempotent terminal replay, password step-up, Manager Action Center composition, and authenticated browser certification. Generic Action Item persistence remains rejected because it does not own terminal business truth.Phase 2 is promoted complete at the product-capability level. The current repository-wide release gate remains red only because an in-progress change to `services/hris/org.service.ts` renamed and expanded the HRIS authority union while the unchanged People page still compares the removed `LOCATION_RESPONSIBILITY` value. The referral program did not modify that workflow. The deployment gate is not waived and must return to green before any release claim.

Evidence: `what-next/referrals/REFERRAL_WAR_ROOM_PHASE_2_PROMOTION_REPORT_2026-07-19.md`.

## lice 21 Audit Evidencelice 21 Audit Evidence

- Current `ActionItem` IDs are in-memory projection identities derived from signal dedupe keys; they have no Prisma persistence or write authority.
- Generic assignment and resolution were rejected for Phase 2 promotion because they do not own or verify a terminal business workflow.
- Payment reconciliation sign-off was selected as the third command because durable `ReconciliationRun` state reaches terminal `SIGNED`.
- The existing source transaction rechecks readiness, provider evidence, open exceptions, open suspense, accounting period, source manifest, independent checker, permission, and fresh authentication.
- Successful source execution writes certificate evidence, sensitive-control audit, ledger audit, `payment.reconciliation.signed`, outbox notification, and close invalidation.
- The command is tenant/provider scoped and must not expand managed-location authority.
- No generic action table or Prisma migration is required for this command.
- Required read-model fields, source identity, deterministic ordering, RBAC, entitlement, redaction, idempotency, stale-source behavior, structured resolution evidence, tests, browser fixture, rollout, and rollback gates are documented.
- Focused baseline verification passes 5 suites/25 tests.

Evidence: `what-next/referrals/DAILY_TRUTH_DURABLE_ACTION_RESOLUTION_FOUNDATION_REPORT_2026-07-19.md`.

## lice 22 Certified Evidencelice 22 Certified Evidence

- The payment-reconciliation-owned contract returns deterministic `AVAILABLE`, `READ_ONLY`, `EMPTY`, or `HIDDEN` state from durable `ReconciliationRun` truth.
- Candidate ordering is `businessDate`, `createdAt`, then `id`, ascending.
- Tenant-wide authority, identity, read/sign RBAC, and enforced read/write module entitlement are established before source exposure.
- Managed-location responsibility receives no provider or reconciliation-run evidence.
- The output is limited to approved provider/date/maker/aggregate evidence with stable source-version and projection hashes.
- Foreign, inactive, malformed, future-dated, stale-status, or inconsistent evidence fails closed.
- Maker-checker conflict is explicit `READ_ONLY`, never `AVAILABLE`.
- Focused verification passes 28/28 tests; the expanded relevant baseline passes 6 suites/53 tests.
- Typecheck and focused ESLint pass; service boundary has 0 active violations; role cockpit is 9/9 ready.
- The module-surface ratchet's two new warn-mode gaps both belong to unrelated `actions/security/step-up-auth.actions.ts`; this service enforces `payment_reconciliation` directly and is not a new gap.
- No sign mutation, UI, Prisma migration, generic Action Item persistence, Phase 3 work, AI, or WhatsApp was added.

Evidence: `what-next/referrals/DAILY_TRUTH_RECONCILIATION_SIGN_OFF_STATE_REPORT_2026-07-19.md`.

## lice 23 Certified Evidencelice 23 Certified Evidence

- The protected action preserves critical `payments.reconciliation.sign` RBAC and carries verified actor, tenant, assurance organization, level, and timestamp provenance into the service.
- `payment_reconciliation` write entitlement is enforced with audited module metadata before mutation.
- The read descriptor and write command share one canonical source-version hash over approved provider, period, maker, total, count, status, and update-time facts.
- The serializable transaction conditionally transitions only the exact current `READY_FOR_SIGNOFF` source with empty terminal evidence.

## Blockers And Risks

- Slice 24 certifies only scheduler-policy preflight shape. It does not authorize cash-shortage detector execution, incident command invocation, worker activation, scheduling, notifications, routes, actions, dashboards, AI, or WhatsApp behavior.
- Slice 7 is certified only as source-owned applied-state completion. It does not authorize backfill, registry persistence, incident creation, worker activation, scheduling, or product surfaces.
- Slice 6 is certified complete only as a read-only event loading and evaluation capability. Scheduling, active registry execution, check-run persistence, and case creation remain prohibited until separately selected.
- The POS cash-shortage definition has explicit service-contract, release-gate, production-preflight, and scheduler-policy ratchets; production runner activation remains blocked and unauthorized until every preflight requirement is certified.
- Only new schema-version-1 close events are eligible prospective evidence. The 135 legacy rows remain untrusted and excluded.
- Existing cash-drawer alerts are transient read-model output with hard-coded currency thresholds and must not be presented as durable Leakage Radar cases.
- Workflow Assurance stable identity, transactional multi-finding persistence, generic lifecycle policy, POS-specific owning-source recheck, terminal command-readiness evidence, protected execution-boundary preflight, idempotent command-wrapper preflight, dormant service-layer POS resolution command wrapper, service-owned resolution source loader, protected server action wrapper, and incident detail product caller are certified. Real browser certification and production release evidence remain blockers before a production Leakage Radar resolution workflow.
- Generic action resolution cannot become truth without rechecking the owning source workflow.
- The configured runtime now reports all 24 migrations applied. Deployment is no longer the active schema blocker, but runtime activation remains prohibited until source, lifecycle, worker, and release gates are separately certified.
- No effective approved production threshold policy exists. The policy table is present but empty; test fixtures are not configuration.
- Approved-policy supersession remains intentionally undefined. An open-ended approved window blocks an overlapping successor rather than mutating approved evidence.
- Repository policy promotion remains held by the unrelated statutory source-artifact hash-verification and expert-approval requirements.
- Empty-database migration bootstrap remains blocked by the pre-existing `20260618154500_repair_accounting_source_links` history assumption; this slice does not waive or repair it.
- Local `npx prisma generate` is currently blocked by a Windows Prisma engine DLL file lock from running Node processes; retry after local dev/test processes release the generated client.
- The existing finance workbench caller remains outside the certified Manager Action Center password-step-up interaction.
- The module inventory remains a coarse file-level report; shared step-up is now explicitly classified as cross-module session assurance rather than a standalone commercial surface.
- Sign-off revocation and supersession remain outside the branch daily-close product UI.
- Branch close still requires an explicit UTC business date; organization-timezone semantics remain a later contract decision.
- Leakage controls must use reviewable exception language, not unsupported fraud accusations.

## Phase 3 / Slice 412 Selected - Inventory Loss Deterministic Review Signal Contract

## Phase 4 / Slice 423 Selected - Accountant Grant Temporal Honesty And Scheduled Revocation

- Slice 423 is selected to reject fully elapsed accountant consent windows and represent future-effective consent as `SCHEDULED` instead of `EXPIRED`.

- Valid browser-local date-time values must reach the action as absolute ISO instants.

## Phase 4 / Slice 423 Certified - Accountant Grant Temporal Honesty And Scheduled Revocation

- Slice 423 is certified for the current worktree after independent review and remediation.
- Fully elapsed consent is rejected before database work; future-effective consent is derived as `SCHEDULED`; exact expiry is derived as `EXPIRED`.

- Delegated resolver and portfolio queries exclude inactive and soft-deleted client organizations.
- The manager submits absolute ISO instants, permits active/scheduled revocation, reports expiry races truthfully, and renders the exact cutoff as machine-readable evidence with local time and zone.
- The report-trust gate now proves executable protected command bindings and delegated resolver predicates with TypeScript AST checks; detached marker strings, unscoped actions, unconditional writes, return-before-guard, legacy mislabeling, and misleading UI evidence fail closed.
